<content>
1: /* editor_menu_input - INTERACT canvas typing + piece.pdl METHOD dispatch.
2:  *
3:  * Usage: editor_menu_input.+x <keycode>
4:  *   key 0     = idle (no-op catch-up; house pal loop shape)
5:  *   printable = append at cursor (INTERACT path)
6:  *   127/8     = backspace
7:  *   10/13     = newline
8:  *   KEY:n via digit / raw method index = NEW / CLEAR / EXIT
9:  *
10:  * Buffer: pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt
11:  * State:  pieces/system/editor_state.txt  (cursor_pos, file_path, last_message)
12:  */
13: #define _GNU_SOURCE
14: #include <stdio.h>
15: #include <stdlib.h>
16: #include <string.h>
17: #include <unistd.h>
18: #include <dirent.h>
19: #include <sys/stat.h>
20: 
21: #define MAX_LINE 2048
22: #define MAX_PATH 4096
23: #define PATH_BUF (MAX_PATH + 256)
24: #define MAX_BUF 65536
25: #define MAX_MENU_ITEMS 32
26: 
27: typedef struct {
28:     char label[160];
29:     char command[128];
30: } MenuItem;
31: 
32: static char project_root[MAX_PATH] = ".";
33: 
34: static void resolve_root(void) {
35:     const char *env = getenv("PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT");
36:     if (env && env[0]) snprintf(project_root, sizeof(project_root), "%s", env);
37: }
38: 
39: static void read_kv_str(const char *path, const char *key, char *out, size_t out_sz) {
40:     out[0] = '\0';
41:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "r");
42:     if (!f) return;
43:     char line[MAX_LINE];
44:     size_t key_len = strlen(key);
45:     while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
46:         if (strncmp(line, key, key_len) == 0 && line[key_len] == '=') {
47:             char *v = line + key_len + 1;
48:             v[strcspn(v, "\n")] = '\0';
49: #pragma GCC diagnostic push
50: #pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wformat-truncation"
51:             snprintf(out, out_sz, "%s", v);
52: #pragma GCC diagnostic pop
53:             break;
54:         }
55:     }
56:     fclose(f);
57: }
58: 
59: static void write_kv(const char *path, const char *key, const char *value) {
60:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "r");
61:     char lines[48][MAX_LINE];
62:     int nlines = 0;
63:     if (f) {
64:         while (nlines < 48 && fgets(lines[nlines], MAX_LINE, f)) nlines++;
65:         fclose(f);
66:     }
67:     size_t key_len = strlen(key);
68:     f = fopen(path, "w");
69:     if (!f) return;
70:     int found = 0;
71:     for (int i = 0; i < nlines; i++) {
72:         if (strncmp(lines[i], key, key_len) == 0 && lines[i][key_len] == '=') {
73:             fprintf(f, "%s=%s\n", key, value);
74:             found = 1;
75:         } else {
76:             fputs(lines[i], f);
77:         }
78:     }
79:     if (!found) fprintf(f, "%s=%s\n", key, value);
80:     fclose(f);
81: }
82: 
83: static char *trim(char *s) {
84:     while (*s == ' ' || *s == '\t') s++;
85:     char *end = s + strlen(s) - 1;
86:     while (end > s && (*end == ' ' || *end == '\t' || *end == '\n' || *end == '\r')) *end-- = '\0';
87:     return s;
88: }
89: 
90: static void bump_screen(void) {
91:     char marker_path[PATH_BUF];
92:     snprintf(marker_path, sizeof(marker_path),
93:              "%s/pieces/display/editor_screen_changed.txt", project_root);
94:     FILE *mf = fopen(marker_path, "a");
95:     if (mf) { fputc('.', mf); fclose(mf); }
96: }
97: 
98: static size_t read_buffer(char *out, size_t out_sz) {
99:     char path[PATH_BUF];
100:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt", project_root);
101:     out[0] = '\0';
102:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "r");
103:     if (!f) return 0;
104:     size_t n = fread(out, 1, out_sz - 1, f);
105:     out[n] = '\0';
106:     fclose(f);
107:     return n;
108: }
109: 
110: static void write_buffer(const char *buf, size_t n) {
111:     char path[PATH_BUF];
112:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt", project_root);
113:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "w");
114:     if (!f) return;
115:     if (n > 0) fwrite(buf, 1, n, f);
116:     fclose(f);
117: }
118: 
119: static int get_cursor(size_t buflen) {
120:     char path[PATH_BUF], raw[64];
121:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_state.txt", project_root);
122:     read_kv_str(path, "cursor_pos", raw, sizeof(raw));
123:     if (!raw[0] || strcmp(raw, "-1") == 0) return (int)buflen;
124:     int p = atoi(raw);
125:     if (p < 0) p = 0;
126:     if ((size_t)p > buflen) p = (int)buflen;
127:     return p;
128: }
129: 
130: static void set_cursor(int pos) {
131:     char path[PATH_BUF], v[32];
132:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_state.txt", project_root);
133:     snprintf(v, sizeof(v), "%d", pos);
134:     write_kv(path, "cursor_pos", v);
135: }
136: 
137: static void set_message(const char *msg) {
138:     char path[PATH_BUF];
139:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_state.txt", project_root);
140:     write_kv(path, "last_message", msg);
141: }
142: 
143: static int load_menu_items(MenuItem *items, int max_items) {
144:     char pdl_path[PATH_BUF];
145:     snprintf(pdl_path, sizeof(pdl_path),
146:              "%s/projects/agy-editor/pieces/editor/piece.pdl", project_root);
147:     FILE *f = fopen(pdl_path, "r");
148:     if (!f) return 0;
149:     char line[MAX_LINE];
150:     int n = 0;
151:     while (n < max_items && fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
152:         if (strncmp(line, "METHOD", 6) != 0) continue;
153:         char *p1 = strchr(line, '|');
154:         if (!p1) continue;
155:         char *p2 = strchr(p1 + 1, '|');
156:         if (!p2) continue;
157:         *p2 = '\0';
158:         char *label = trim(p1 + 1);
159:         char *command = trim(p2 + 1);
160:         snprintf(items[n].label, sizeof(items[n].label), "%s", label);
161:         snprintf(items[n].command, sizeof(items[n].command), "%s", command);
162:         n++;
163:     }
164:     fclose(f);
165:     return n;
166: }
167: 
168: static void do_new_file(void) {
169:     write_buffer("", 0);
170:     set_cursor(0);
171:     char path[PATH_BUF];
172:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/editor_state.txt", project_root);
173:     write_kv(path, "file_path", "docs/untitled.txt");
174:     set_message("NEW FILE — empty buffer.");
175: }
176: 
177: static void do_clear(void) {
178:     write_buffer("", 0);
179:     set_cursor(0);
180:     set_message("CLEAR FILE — buffer cleared.");
181: }
182: 
183: static void do_exit(void) {
184:     char path[PATH_BUF];
185:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/quit_flag.txt", project_root);
186:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "w");
187:     if (f) { fputs("1\n", f); fclose(f); }
188:     set_message("EXIT — quit flag set (Ctrl+C if still live).");
189: }
190: 
191: /* Read house_root.txt from the editor session to find the file-menu dir. */
192: static int read_house_root(char *out, size_t out_sz) {
193:     char path[PATH_BUF];
194:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/pieces/system/house_root.txt", project_root);
195:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "r");
196:     if (!f) return -1;
197:     if (!fgets(out, (int)out_sz, f)) { fclose(f); return -1; }
198:     fclose(f);
199:     size_t ln = strlen(out);
200:     while (ln > 0 && (out[ln-1] == '\n' || out[ln-1] == '\r')) out[--ln] = '\0';
201:     return (out[0]) ? 0 : -1;
202: }
203: 
204: /* Find the file-menu widget's session dir by scanning for focus.txt that
205:  * points back to this editor session. Uses house_root.txt to locate the
206:  * file-menu sessions directory. Returns 0 if found, -1 if not. */
207: static int find_widget_session(char *out, size_t out_sz) {
208:     char house[PATH_BUF];
209:     if (read_house_root(house, sizeof(house)) != 0) return -1;
210:     char base[PATH_BUF];
211:     snprintf(base, sizeof(base), "%s/&.widgits/file-menu/pieces/sessions", house);
212:     DIR *d = opendir(base);
213:     if (!d) return -1;
214:     char session_line[MAX_LINE];
215:     snprintf(session_line, sizeof(session_line), "session_root=%s", project_root);
216:     int found = -1;
217:     struct dirent *entry;
218:     while ((entry = readdir(d))) {
219:         if (entry->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
220:         char focus_path[PATH_BUF];
221:         snprintf(focus_path, sizeof(focus_path), "%s/%s/pieces/system/focus.txt", base, entry->d_name);
222:         FILE *f = fopen(focus_path, "r");
223:         if (!f) continue;
224:         char line[MAX_LINE];
225:         while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
226:             line[strcspn(line, "\n")] = '\0';
227:             if (strcmp(line, session_line) == 0) {
228:                 snprintf(out, out_sz, "%s/%s", base, entry->d_name);
229:                 found = 0;
230:                 fclose(f);
231:                 closedir(d);
232:                 return found;
233:             }
234:         }
235:         fclose(f);
236:     }
237:     closedir(d);
238:     return found;
239: }
240: 
241: /* FM handler — bridge FILE MENU keypress to the file-menu widget.
242:  * Finds the widget's session dir and writes key "1" (menu activate)
243:  * to its interact_relay.txt so the widget's PAL loop processes it. */
244: static void do_fm(void) {
245:     char widget_root[PATH_BUF];
246:     if (find_widget_session(widget_root, sizeof(widget_root)) != 0) {
247:         set_message("FM — no widget session (start app with button.sh run)");
248:         return;
249:     }
250:     char relay[PATH_BUF];
251:     snprintf(relay, sizeof(relay), "%s/pieces/apps/player_app/interact_relay.txt", widget_root);
252:     FILE *rf = fopen(relay, "a");
253:     if (!rf) {
254:         set_message("FM — cannot reach widget relay");
255:         return;
256:     }
257:     fprintf(rf, "1\n");
258:     fclose(rf);
259:     set_message("FILE MENU — signal sent to widget");
260: }
261: 
262: /* Insert one byte at cursor. */
263: static void insert_char(char ch) {
264:     char buf[MAX_BUF];
265:     size_t blen = read_buffer(buf, sizeof(buf));
266:     if (blen + 1 >= sizeof(buf)) return;
267:     int cur = get_cursor(blen);
268:     memmove(buf + cur + 1, buf + cur, blen - (size_t)cur);
269:     buf[cur] = ch;
270:     blen++;
271:     buf[blen] = '\0';
272:     write_buffer(buf, blen);
273:     set_cursor(cur + 1);
274: }
275: 
276: static void do_backspace(void) {
277:     char buf[MAX_BUF];
278:     size_t blen = read_buffer(buf, sizeof(buf));
279:     int cur = get_cursor(blen);
280:     if (cur <= 0) return;
281:     memmove(buf + cur - 1, buf + cur, blen - (size_t)cur);
282:     blen--;
283:     buf[blen] = '\0';
284:     write_buffer(buf, blen);
285:     set_cursor(cur - 1);
286: }
287: 
288: /* Arrow keys while INTERACT: move cursor horizontally */
289: static void move_cursor(int delta) {
290:     char buf[MAX_BUF];
291:     size_t blen = read_buffer(buf, sizeof(buf));
292:     int cur = get_cursor(blen) + delta;
293:     if (cur < 0) cur = 0;
294:     if ((size_t)cur > blen) cur = (int)blen;
295:     set_cursor(cur);
296: }
297: 
298: /* Up/down: same column on previous/next line (clamp to line length). */
299: static void move_cursor_line(int dir) {
300:     char buf[MAX_BUF];
301:     size_t blen = read_buffer(buf, sizeof(buf));
302:     int cur = get_cursor(blen);
303:     if (blen == 0) return;
304: 
305:     /* Line start of current line */
306:     int line_start = cur;
307:     while (line_start > 0 && buf[line_start - 1] != '\n') line_start--;
308:     int col = cur - line_start;
309: 
310:     if (dir < 0) {
311:         /* previous line */
312:         if (line_start == 0) return; /* already top */
313:         int prev_end = line_start - 1; /* the \n before current line */
314:         int prev_start = prev_end;
315:         while (prev_start > 0 && buf[prev_start - 1] != '\n') prev_start--;
316:         int prev_len = prev_end - prev_start;
317:         int new_col = col < prev_len ? col : prev_len;
318:         set_cursor(prev_start + new_col);
319:     } else {
320:         /* next line */
321:         int line_end = cur;
322:         while ((size_t)line_end < blen && buf[line_end] != '\n') line_end++;
323:         if ((size_t)line_end >= blen) return; /* already last line, no trailing \n past end */
324:         int next_start = line_end + 1;
325:         int next_end = next_start;
326:         while ((size_t)next_end < blen && buf[next_end] != '\n') next_end++;
327:         int next_len = next_end - next_start;
328:         int new_col = col < next_len ? col : next_len;
329:         set_cursor(next_start + new_col);
330:     }
331: }
332: 
333: /* 1 when chtpm has an engaged INTERACT/cli_io (active_index != -1).
334:  * Written by export_active_index() in chtpm_parser_pal. */
335: static int is_interact_typing(void) {
336:     char path[PATH_BUF], line[16];
337:     snprintf(path, sizeof(path),
338:              "%s/pieces/display/active_gui_is_typing.txt", project_root);
339:     FILE *f = fopen(path, "r");
340:     if (!f) return 0;
341:     if (!fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) { fclose(f); return 0; }
342:     fclose(f);
343:     return atoi(line) != 0;
344: }
345: 
346: int main(int argc, char **argv) {
347:     if (argc < 2) return 1;
348:     resolve_root();
349: 
350:     int key = atoi(argv[1]);
351:     if (key == 0) {
352:         /* idle: drain widget/file-menu cmd inbox (LOAD/SAVE/NEW).
353:          * Separate op keeps harness able to call it explicitly too. */
354:         char cmd[PATH_BUF * 2];
355:         snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd),
356:                  "cd '%s' && ./ops/+x/editor_widget_cmds.+x 8 >/dev/null 2>&1",
357:                  project_root);
358:         system(cmd);
359:         return 0;
360:     }
361: 
362:     int typing = is_interact_typing();
363: 
364:     /* Method dispatch only when NOT in INTERACT (digits are text then).
365:      * Supports both ASCII digits ('2'-'5') and raw integers (2-5) as
366:      * 1-based method indices matching KEY:1-5 in piece.pdl. */
367:     if (!typing) {
368:         MenuItem items[MAX_MENU_ITEMS];
369:         int item_count = load_menu_items(items, MAX_MENU_ITEMS);
370:         int method_index = 0;
371: 
372:         if (key >= '1' && key <= '9')
373:             method_index = key - '0';
374:         else if (key >= 1 && key <= 99)
375:             method_index = key;
376: 
377:         if (method_index >= 1 && method_index <= item_count) {
378:             const char *cmd = items[method_index - 1].command;
379:             if (strcmp(cmd, "NEW") == 0) do_new_file();
380:             else if (strcmp(cmd, "CLEAR") == 0) do_clear();
381:             else if (strcmp(cmd, "EXIT") == 0) do_exit();
382:             else if (strcmp(cmd, "FM") == 0) do_fm();
383:             else set_message("Unknown command.");
384:             bump_screen();
385:             return 0;
386:         }
387:         return 0;
388:     }
389: 
390:     /* INTERACT canvas keys */
391:     if (key >= 32 && key <= 126) {
392:         insert_char((char)key);
393:         bump_screen();
394:         return 0;
395:     }
396:     if (key == 127 || key == 8) {
397:         do_backspace();
398:         bump_screen();
399:         return 0;
400:     }
401:     if (key == 10 || key == 13) {
402:         insert_char('\n');
403:         bump_screen();
404:         return 0;
405:     }
406:     /* arrows: left/right char, up/down line (same column) */
407:     if (key == 1000) { move_cursor(-1); bump_screen(); return 0; }      /* LEFT */
408:     if (key == 1001) { move_cursor(+1); bump_screen(); return 0; }      /* RIGHT */
409:     if (key == 1002) { move_cursor_line(-1); bump_screen(); return 0; } /* UP */
410:     if (key == 1003) { move_cursor_line(+1); bump_screen(); return 0; } /* DOWN */
411: 
412:     return 0;
413: }

1: #!/bin/bash
2: # button.sh - text-editor-xyz app launcher
3: #
4: # Architecture (per pal-standards §36):
5: #   Editor and file-menu widget are SEPARATE PROGRAMS, each with their
6: #   own session directory, system binaries, ops, and PAL loops.
7: #   They communicate via file-mediated widget cmd bus (inbox/status).
8: #   App launcher starts both simultaneously and kills both on exit.
9: #
10: #   Editor: foreground session (owns TTY, has keyboard_input)
11: #   Widget: background session (widget profile, TTY→/dev/null)
12: ACTION="${1:-help}"
13: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
14: HOUSE_DIR="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
15: 
16: EDITOR_DIR="$(ls -d "$HOUSE_DIR/102."*"editor"* 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
17: FM_DIR="$(ls -d "$HOUSE_DIR/&.widgits/file-menu"* 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
18: 
19: err() { echo "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
20: [ -n "$EDITOR_DIR" ] && [ -d "$EDITOR_DIR" ] || err "Editor not found in $HOUSE_DIR"
21: [ -n "$FM_DIR" ] && [ -d "$FM_DIR" ] || err "File-menu widget not found in $HOUSE_DIR"
22: 
23: SESSION_ID="$(date +%s)-$$"
24: EDITOR_SESSION="/tmp/.text-editor-xyz-editor-$SESSION_ID"
25: 
26: run_app() {
27:     # ── 1. Create EDITOR session directory ──
28:     mkdir -p "$EDITOR_SESSION/pieces/system" "$EDITOR_SESSION/pieces/display" \
29:              "$EDITOR_SESSION/pieces/apps/player_app" "$EDITOR_SESSION/pieces/keyboard" \
30:              "$EDITOR_SESSION/projects/agy-editor/manager" \
31:              "$EDITOR_SESSION/docs"
32: 
33:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/system" "$EDITOR_SESSION/system"
34:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/ops" "$EDITOR_SESSION/ops"
35:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/pal" "$EDITOR_SESSION/pal"
36:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/default_op.txt" "$EDITOR_SESSION/default_op.txt"
37:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/pieces/chtpm" "$EDITOR_SESSION/pieces/chtpm"
38:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/projects/agy-editor/pieces" "$EDITOR_SESSION/projects/agy-editor/pieces"
39:     ln -sfn "$EDITOR_DIR/docs" "$EDITOR_SESSION/docs"
40: 
41:     cd "$EDITOR_SESSION"
42: 
43:     # ── 2. Init editor state ──
44:     : > pieces/apps/player_app/interact_relay.txt
45:     : > pieces/keyboard/history.txt
46:     : > pieces/display/editor_screen_changed.txt
47:     : > pieces/display/frame_changed.txt
48:     : > projects/agy-editor/manager/gui_state.txt
49: 
50:     if [ ! -f pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt ]; then
51:         printf 'hi   \360\237\230\205 \360\237\230\206 \360\237\220\247\n' > pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt
52:     fi
53:     cat > pieces/system/editor_state.txt << 'EOF'
54: file_path=docs/untitled.txt
55: cursor_pos=-1
56: last_message=text-editor-xyz ready. F4 for FILE MENU.
57: EOF
58:     cat > pieces/apps/player_app/state.txt << 'EOSTATE'
59: module_path=system/prisc+x pal/main_loop_chtpm.pal
60: project_id=agy-editor
61: active_target_id=editor
62: EOSTATE
63: 
64:     export PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT="$EDITOR_SESSION"
65:     export PRISC_PROJECT_ID="agy-editor"
66: 
67:     # ── 3. Set up widget cmd bus (editor's inbox for widget commands) ──
68:     mkdir -p pieces/system/widget_cmds
69:     : > pieces/system/widget_cmds/inbox.txt
70:     : > pieces/system/widget_cmds/status.txt
71: 
72:     # ── 4. Compose initial editor frame ──
73:     if [ -x ./ops/+x/editor_compose_frame.+x ]; then
74:         PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT="$EDITOR_SESSION" \
75:             ./ops/+x/editor_compose_frame.+x >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
76:     fi
77: 
78:     # ── 5. Write house root for widget session discovery ──
79:     echo "$HOUSE_DIR" > pieces/system/house_root.txt
80: 
81:     # ── 6. Start editor daemons (background) ──
82:     ./system/renderer &
83:     RENDERER_PID=$!
84:     ./system/chtpm_parser_pal pieces/chtpm/layouts/editor.chtpm >/dev/null 2>&1 &
85:     CHTPM_PID=$!
86: 
87:     # ── 7. Start widget cmd drainer (background) ──
88:     WIDGET_CMDS_PID=""
89:     if [ -x "$EDITOR_DIR/ops/+x/editor_widget_cmds.+x" ]; then
90:         (
91:             cd "$EDITOR_SESSION"
92:             while [ -f pieces/system/widget_cmds/inbox.txt ]; do
93:                 PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT="$EDITOR_SESSION" \
94:                     "$EDITOR_DIR/ops/+x/editor_widget_cmds.+x" 8 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
95:                 sleep 0.2
96:             done
97:         ) &
98:         WIDGET_CMDS_PID=$!
99:     fi
100: 
101:     # ── 8. Start file-menu widget (background, widget profile, TTY→/dev/null)
102:     #      gl_mirror opens the GL window; keyboard forwarded to interact_relay.txt ──
103:     cd "$FM_DIR"
104:     RUN_PROFILE=widget \
105:         bash "$FM_DIR/button.sh" run-widget "$EDITOR_SESSION" >/dev/null 2>&1 &
106:     WIDGET_PID=$!
107: 
108:     cd "$EDITOR_SESSION"
109: 
110:     # ── 9. Cleanup trap ──
111:     kill_own_module() {
112:         local pid cwd
113:         for pid in $(pgrep -f "system/prisc\+x" 2>/dev/null); do
114:             cwd="$(readlink -f "/proc/$pid/cwd" 2>/dev/null)"
115:             if [ "$cwd" = "$EDITOR_SESSION" ]; then
116:                 kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null
117:             fi
118:         done
119:     }
120: 
121:     cleanup() {
122:         kill "$WIDGET_CMDS_PID" "$RENDERER_PID" "$CHTPM_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
123:         kill "$WIDGET_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
124:         kill_own_module
125:         mkdir -p "$SCRIPT_DIR/sessions"
126:         if [ -f pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt ]; then
127:             cp pieces/system/editor_buffer.txt "$SCRIPT_DIR/sessions/autosave.txt" 2>/dev/null || true
128:         fi
129:         rm -rf "$EDITOR_SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
130:     }
131:     trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM
132: 
133:     # ── 10. Keyboard input (foreground, blocking) ──
134:     : > pieces/apps/player_app/history.txt
135:     ./system/keyboard_input
136: 
137:     cleanup
138: }
139: 
140: case "$ACTION" in
141:     compile|c|build)
142:         echo "text-editor-xyz: checking dependencies..."
143:         echo "  editor: $EDITOR_DIR"
144:         echo "  file-menu: $FM_DIR"
145:         echo "--- Editor ---"
146:         for b in system/prisc+x system/keyboard_input system/renderer \
147:                  system/chtpm_parser_pal; do
148:             [ -x "$EDITOR_DIR/$b" ] && echo "OK   $b" || echo "MISS $b"
149:         done
150:         for b in ops/+x/editor_menu_input.+x ops/+x/editor_compose_frame.+x \
151:                  ops/+x/editor_widget_cmds.+x; do
152:             [ -x "$EDITOR_DIR/$b" ] && echo "OK   $b" || echo "MISS $b"
153:         done
154:         echo "--- File-menu widget ---"
155:         for b in system/prisc+x system/chtpm_parser_pal; do
156:             [ -x "$FM_DIR/$b" ] && echo "OK   $b" || echo "MISS $b"
157:         done
158:         for b in ops/+x/fm_set_focus.+x ops/+x/fm_enqueue_cmd.+x \
159:                  ops/+x/fm_compose_frame.+x ops/+x/fm_menu_input.+x; do
160:             [ -x "$FM_DIR/$b" ] && echo "OK   $b" || echo "MISS $b"
161:         done
162:         echo "--- Layouts ---"
163:         [ -f "$FM_DIR/pieces/chtpm/layouts/file-menu.chtpm" ] && echo "OK   file-menu.chtpm" || echo "MISS file-menu.chtpm"
164:         [ -f "$FM_DIR/pal/main_loop_chtpm.pal" ] && echo "OK   main_loop_chtpm.pal" || echo "MISS main_loop_chtpm.pal"
165:         [ -f "$EDITOR_DIR/pieces/chtpm/layouts/editor.chtpm" ] && echo "OK   editor.chtpm" || echo "MISS editor.chtpm"
166:         [ -f "$EDITOR_DIR/pal/main_loop_chtpm.pal" ] && echo "OK   editor main_loop_chtpm.pal" || echo "MISS editor main_loop_chtpm.pal"
167:         ;;
168:     run|r|start)
169:         run_app
170:         ;;
171:     kill|k|stop)
172:         pkill -f "system/keyboard_input" 2>/dev/null
173:         pkill -f "system/renderer" 2>/dev/null
174:         pkill -f "system/prisc\+x" 2>/dev/null
175:         pkill -f "system/chtpm_parser_pal" 2>/dev/null
176:         rm -rf /tmp/.text-editor-xyz-* 2>/dev/null
177:         echo "done"
178:         ;;
179:     help|h|-h|--help)
180:         echo "text-editor-xyz — Text editor with file-menu widget GL window"
181:         echo ""
182:         echo "Architecture (pal-standards §36):"
183:         echo "  Editor and file-menu widget are SEPARATE PROGRAMS."
184:         echo "  Editor: foreground (owns TTY, INTERACT canvas)."
185:         echo "  Widget: background (GL window, no TTY)."
186:         echo "  Communication: file-mediated widget cmd bus (inbox.txt)."
187:         echo ""
188:         echo "Usage: ./button.sh <action>"
189:         echo "  compile      - Check dependencies"
190:         echo "  run          - Launch editor + file-menu widget"
191:         echo "  kill         - Kill processes"
192:         echo "  help         - This help"
193:         ;;
194:     *)
195:         echo "Unknown: $ACTION (try ./button.sh help)"
196:         exit 1
197:         ;;
198: esac

1: #!/bin/bash
2: # button.sh - file-menu widget (ops + GL widget chrome)
3: # House pattern: session-isolated UI state + shared system/ops/pal.
4: # run-widget: GL-primary window for file operations against a focused editor.
5: # Profile: RUN_PROFILE=app (default) or RUN_PROFILE=widget
6: #   app mode:    ascii_renderer=1, keyboard_input foreground
7: #   widget mode: ascii_renderer=0, no keyboard_input, gl_mirror for GL + input
8: ACTION="${1:-help}"
9: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
10: 
11: run_widget_session() {
12:     local FOCUS_SESSION="${1:-}"
13:     local PROFILE="${RUN_PROFILE:-app}"
14:     SESSION_ID="$(date +%s)-$$"
15:     SESSION_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/pieces/sessions/$SESSION_ID"
16:     mkdir -p "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/system" "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/display" \
17:              "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/apps/player_app" "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/keyboard" \
18:              "$SESSION_DIR/projects/file-menu/manager"
19: 
20:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/system" "$SESSION_DIR/system" 2>/dev/null || true
21:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/ops" "$SESSION_DIR/ops" 2>/dev/null || true
22:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/pal" "$SESSION_DIR/pal" 2>/dev/null || true
23:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/pieces/chtpm" "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/chtpm" 2>/dev/null || true
24:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/pieces/registry" "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/registry" 2>/dev/null || true
25:     mkdir -p "$SESSION_DIR/projects/file-menu/pieces"
26:     ln -sfn "$SCRIPT_DIR/projects/file-menu/pieces/file-menu" \
27:             "$SESSION_DIR/projects/file-menu/pieces/file-menu" 2>/dev/null || true
28: 
29:     cd "$SESSION_DIR"
30:     : > pieces/apps/player_app/interact_relay.txt
31:     : > pieces/keyboard/history.txt
32:     : > pieces/display/frame_changed.txt
33:     : > pieces/system/fm_state.txt
34:     cat > pieces/system/fm_state.txt << 'EOF'
35: mode=main_menu
36: path_buffer=
37: EOF
38:     cat > pieces/apps/player_app/state.txt << 'EOSTATE'
39: module_path=system/prisc+x pal/main_loop_chtpm.pal
40: project_id=file-menu
41: active_target_id=file-menu
42: EOSTATE
43: 
44:     export PRISC_PROJECT_ROOT="$SESSION_DIR"
45:     export PRISC_PROJECT_ID="file-menu"
46: 
47:     if [ -n "$FOCUS_SESSION" ] && [ -x ./ops/+x/fm_set_focus.+x ]; then
48:         ./ops/+x/fm_set_focus.+x "$SESSION_DIR/pieces/system" "$FOCUS_SESSION" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
49:     fi
50: 
51:     if [ -x ./ops/+x/fm_compose_frame.+x ]; then
52:         ./ops/+x/fm_compose_frame.+x >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
53:     fi
54: 
55:     if [ "$PROFILE" = "widget" ]; then
56:         # WIDGET MODE: GL-primary, no ASCII renderer, no keyboard_input
57:         # chtpm_parser_pal runs the PAL loop; gl_mirror provides GL window + keyboard
58:         # chtpm_rgb_render converts current_frame.txt → rgb_frame.raw for gl_mirror
59:         GL_PID=""
60:         RGB_PID=""
61:         if [ -z "$NO_GL" ] && [ -x ./system/gl_mirror ]; then
62:             ./system/gl_mirror >/dev/null 2>&1 &
63:             GL_PID=$!
64:         fi
65:         if [ -z "$NO_GL" ] && [ -x ./system/chtpm_rgb_render ]; then
66:             ./system/chtpm_rgb_render >/dev/null 2>&1 &
67:             RGB_PID=$!
68:         fi
69:         ./system/chtpm_parser_pal pieces/chtpm/layouts/file-menu.chtpm >/dev/null 2>&1 &
70:         CHTPM_PID=$!
71: 
72:         kill_own_module() {
73:             local pid cwd
74:             for pid in $(pgrep -f "system/prisc\+x" 2>/dev/null); do
75:                 cwd="$(readlink -f "/proc/$pid/cwd" 2>/dev/null)"
76:                 if [ "$cwd" = "$SESSION_DIR" ]; then
77:                     kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null
78:                 fi
79:             done
80:         }
81:         trap 'kill "$CHTPM_PID" "$GL_PID" "$RGB_PID" 2>/dev/null; kill_own_module; rm -rf "$SESSION_DIR"' EXIT INT TERM
82: 
83:         wait "$CHTPM_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
84:         kill "$GL_PID" "$RGB_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
85:     else
86:         # APP MODE: ASCII renderer + keyboard_input (same-TTY)
87:         ./system/renderer &
88:         RENDERER_PID=$!
89:         ./system/chtpm_parser_pal pieces/chtpm/layouts/file-menu.chtpm >/dev/null 2>&1 &
90:         CHTPM_PID=$!
91: 
92:         kill_own_module() {
93:             local pid cwd
94:             for pid in $(pgrep -f "system/prisc\+x" 2>/dev/null); do
95:                 cwd="$(readlink -f "/proc/$pid/cwd" 2>/dev/null)"
96:                 if [ "$cwd" = "$SESSION_DIR" ]; then
97:                     kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null
98:                 fi
99:             done
100:         }
101: 
102:         trap 'kill "$RENDERER_PID" "$CHTPM_PID" 2>/dev/null; kill_own_module; rm -rf "$SESSION_DIR"' EXIT INT TERM
103: 
104:         : > pieces/apps/player_app/history.txt
105:         ./system/keyboard_input
106: 
107:         kill "$RENDERER_PID" "$CHTPM_PID" 2>/dev/null
108:         kill_own_module
109:     fi
110: }
111: 
112: case "$ACTION" in
113:     compile|c|build)
114:         bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/scripts/build.sh"
115:         ;;
116:     run-widget|widget|w)
117:         # GL-primary file-menu window focused on an editor session
118:         FOCUS="${2:-}"
119:         if [ -z "$FOCUS" ]; then
120:             LF="$SCRIPT_DIR/pieces/sessions/live-focus/focus.txt"
121:             if [ -f "$LF" ]; then
122:                 FOCUS="$(grep '^session_root=' "$LF" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2-)"
123:             fi
124:         fi
125:         if [ -z "$FOCUS" ]; then
126:             echo "Usage: ./button.sh run-widget <editor_session_root>"

162:     help|h|-h|--help)
163:         echo "file-menu widget — GL window for file operations"
164:         echo ""
165:         echo "Usage: ./button.sh <action> [args]"
